# IMPERIA COLD WAR — Annual Union orders in the native Decisions menu.
COLDWAR_ORD_refresh_draft = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_commit_draft = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
			var:COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = { COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 1 }
			}
			var:COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = { COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_rifles = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 2 }
			}
			var:COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = { COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_command = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 3 }
			}

		if = {

COLDWAR_ORD_snapshot_proposals = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SOV
			COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
			var:COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = { COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes }
		}
		var:COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = {
			SOV = {
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_rifles = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_command = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_command_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_utility = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_utility_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_heavy_utility = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_mbt = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_tank = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_apc = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_apc_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_ifv = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_towed_artillery = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_sp_artillery = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_mlrs = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_aa = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_aa_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_spaa = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_at = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_heavy_at = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_fighter = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_multirole = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_cas = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_multirole = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_air_superiority = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_cas = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cas_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_air_transport = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_attack_helicopter = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_transport_helicopter = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_patrol = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_corvette = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_frigate = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_destroyer = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_cruiser = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_carrier = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_attack_submarine = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_missile_submarine = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_convoy = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_guided_missile = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_ballistic_missile = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_sam_missile = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_artillery_class = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_civilian = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_serial }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_military = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_military_serial }

				set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_dockyard = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_serial }
			}
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_initialised }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_initialised
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 5 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity = 300 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_step = 100 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction = 0 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_stage = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { tag = SOV }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = UKR.id }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_bootstrap = {
	if = {
		limit = { IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes exists = yes tag = SOV }
		set_global_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_enabled
		COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_legacy_migrated }
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_legacy_migrated
			if = {
				limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_union_plan }
				remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_union_plan
			}
			clr_global_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_year_running
			clr_global_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_year_won
		}
		SOV = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		UKR = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		BLR = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		EST = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		LAT = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		LIT = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		ARM = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		AZE = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		GEO = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		KAZ = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		KYR = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		TAJ = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		TRK = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		UZB = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
		MLV = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_legacy_migrated }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_legacy_migrated
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_civ }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_civ
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_arms }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_arms
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_power }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_power
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_agri }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_agri
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_roads }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_roads
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_dock }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_dock
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_oil }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_oil
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_deliver }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_deliver
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_discipline }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_discipline
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_steel }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_steel
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_cotton }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_cotton
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_virgin }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_virgin
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_orchards }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_target_orchards
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_target_active
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_civ }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_civ
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_arms }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_arms
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_power }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_power
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_nuclear }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_nuclear
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_agri }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_agri
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_roads }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_roads
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_dock }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_dock
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_deliver }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_deliver
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_komsomol }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_komsomol
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_directive_active
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_dir_pending
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall }
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_member = yes
			SOV = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_remaining = SOV.days_mission_timeout@COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall days = COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_remaining }
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_sync_policies = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_member = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_cheap_directives }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_directive_staff }
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_directive_staff
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_directive_staff }
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_directive_staff
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_member = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_peoples_control }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_production_audit }
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_production_audit
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_production_audit }
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_production_audit
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_member = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_party_control }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_control_commission }
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_control_commission
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_control_commission }
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_control_commission
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_member = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_counter_unlocked }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_counter_plan }
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_counter_plan
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_counter_plan }
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_counter_plan
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_member = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_procurement_agents }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_procurement_agents }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_policy_left = days_mission_timeout@COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_procurement_agents }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_procurement_agents days = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_left }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_procurement_agents }
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_procurement_agents
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_member = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_overtime }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_overtime }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_policy_left = days_mission_timeout@COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_overtime }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_overtime days = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_left }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_overtime }
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_overtime
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_daily = {
	if = {
		limit = { IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes has_global_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_enabled }
		COLDWAR_ORD_initialise_country = yes
		COLDWAR_ORD_retire_legacy = yes
		COLDWAR_ORD_sync_policies = yes
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_refresh_draft = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_member = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_member_seen }
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_member_seen
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_all = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_member = no has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_member_seen }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_member_seen
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_all = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = { COLDWAR_ORD_republic = no COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = no }
				}
				COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_rifles = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 2 }
				}
				COLDWAR_ORD_measure_rifles = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_rifles = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 1 }
				OR = { COLDWAR_ORD_republic = no COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = no }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 0 }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_rifles
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_rifles
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 1 }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
			multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_fraction }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 1 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_rifles }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 0 }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_rifles
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state > 1 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_rifles }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_close_rifles = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = { COLDWAR_ORD_republic = no COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = no }
				}
				COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_command = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state = 2 }
				}
				COLDWAR_ORD_measure_command = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_command = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state = 1 }
				OR = { COLDWAR_ORD_republic = no COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = no }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state = 0 }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_command
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_command
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state = 1 }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
			multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_command_fraction }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state = 1 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_command }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state = 0 }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_command
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state > 1 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_command }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_close_command = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = { COLDWAR_ORD_republic = no COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = no }
				}
				COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_utility = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 2 }
				}
				COLDWAR_ORD_measure_utility = yes
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_utility = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 1 }
				OR = { COLDWAR_ORD_republic = no COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = no }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 0 }
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_utility
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_utility
		}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_rifles = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_rifles = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 1 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_rifles = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_rifles value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_rifles
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_rifles = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_rifles
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_rifles
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_rifles value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_rifles
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_rifles
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_rifles = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_rifles = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_rifles = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_rifles = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_rifles = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_rifles
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_rifles
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_rifles = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_rifles
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_rifles = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_rifles = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_rifles
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_rifles
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_rifles value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_rifles
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_rifles = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_rifles = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_rifles
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_rifles
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_rifles value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_rifles
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_rifles = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_rifles = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_rifles = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_rifles = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_rifles = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_rifles = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_rifles = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_rifles
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_rifles
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_rifles
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_rifles = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_rifles = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@infantry_weapons_type value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@infantry_weapons_type }
		send_equipment = { type = infantry_weapons_type amount = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@infantry_weapons_type }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_rifles = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_rifles = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 1 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_rifles = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_command = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_command = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 2 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_command_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_command_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_command_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_command = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_command value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_command
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_command = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_command_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_command_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_command_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_command_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_command_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_command
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_command
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_command value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_command
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_command
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_command_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {

	AZE = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_command = yes
		}
	}
	GEO = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_command = yes
		}
	}
	KAZ = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_command = yes
		}
	}
	KYR = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_command = yes
		}
	}
	TAJ = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_command = yes
		}
	}
	TRK = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_command = yes
		}
	}
	UZB = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_command = yes
		}
	}
	MLV = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_command = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_utility = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_utility = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 3 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_utility = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_utility value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_utility
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_utility = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_utility_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_utility_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_utility
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_utility
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_utility value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_utility
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_utility
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_utility = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_utility = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_utility_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_utility = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_utility = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_utility = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_utility_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_utility
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_utility
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_utility = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_utility
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_utility = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_utility = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_utility
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_utility
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_utility value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_utility
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_utility = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_utility = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_utility
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_utility
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_utility value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_utility
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_utility = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_utility = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_utility = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_utility = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_utility = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_utility = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_utility = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_utility
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_utility
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_utility
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_utility = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_utility = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@util_vehicle_type value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@util_vehicle_type }
		send_equipment = { type = util_vehicle_type amount = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@util_vehicle_type }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_utility = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_utility = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_utility_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 3 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_utility_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_utility_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_utility = yes
	}
}

		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_heavy_utility
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_heavy_utility value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_heavy_utility

			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }

COLDWAR_ORD_close_heavy_utility = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_heavy_utility = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_heavy_utility = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_heavy_utility = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_heavy_utility = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_heavy_utility
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_heavy_utility
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_heavy_utility
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_heavy_utility = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_heavy_utility = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@heavy_util_vehicle_type value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@heavy_util_vehicle_type }
		send_equipment = { type = heavy_util_vehicle_type amount = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@heavy_util_vehicle_type }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_heavy_utility = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_heavy_utility = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 4 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_utility_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_heavy_utility = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_mbt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_mbt = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 5 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_mbt = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_mbt value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_mbt
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_mbt = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_mbt
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_mbt
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_mbt value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_mbt
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_mbt
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_mbt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_mbt = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_mbt = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_mbt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_mbt = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_mbt
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_mbt
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_mbt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_mbt
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_mbt = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_mbt = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_mbt
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_mbt
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_mbt value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_mbt
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_mbt = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_mbt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_mbt
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_mbt
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_mbt value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_mbt
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_mbt = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_mbt = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_mbt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_mbt = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_mbt = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_mbt = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_mbt = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_mbt
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_mbt
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_mbt
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_mbt = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_mbt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@medium_tank_chassis value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_chassis }
		send_equipment = { type = medium_tank_chassis amount = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_chassis }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_mbt = yes
	}
}

	}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_light_tank = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_light_tank = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 6 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_tank = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_tank value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_tank
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_tank = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_tank
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_tank
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_tank value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_tank
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_tank
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_light_tank = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV

			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_tank = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_light_tank = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_tank = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_tank
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_tank
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_light_tank = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_tank
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_tank = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_light_tank = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_tank
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_tank
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_tank value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_tank
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_light_tank = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_light_tank = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_tank
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_tank
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_tank value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_tank
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_light_tank = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_light_tank = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_light_tank = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_light_tank = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_light_tank = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_tank = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_light_tank = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_tank
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_tank
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_tank
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_light_tank = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_light_tank = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@medium_tank_destroyer_chassis value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_destroyer_chassis }
		send_equipment = { type = medium_tank_destroyer_chassis amount = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_destroyer_chassis }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_tank = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_light_tank = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 6 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_tank_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_tank = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_apc = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_apc = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 7 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_apc = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_apc value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_apc
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_apc = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_apc_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_apc_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_apc
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_apc
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_apc value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_apc
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_apc
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_apc = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_apc = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_apc_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_apc = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_apc = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_apc = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_apc_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_apc
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_apc
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_apc = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_apc
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_apc = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_apc = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_apc
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_apc
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_apc value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_apc
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_apc = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_apc = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_apc
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_apc
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_apc value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_apc
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_apc = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_apc = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_apc = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_apc = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_apc = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_apc = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_apc = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_apc
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_apc
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_apc
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_apc = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_apc = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@medium_tank_amphibious_chassis value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_amphibious_chassis }
		send_equipment = { type = medium_tank_amphibious_chassis amount = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_amphibious_chassis }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_apc_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_apc_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_apc = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_ifv = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_ifv = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 8 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_ifv = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_ifv value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_ifv
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_ifv = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_ifv
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_ifv
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_ifv value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_ifv
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_ifv
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_ifv = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_ifv = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_ifv = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_ifv = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_ifv = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_ifv
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_ifv
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_ifv = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_ifv
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_ifv = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_ifv = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_ifv
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_ifv
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_ifv value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_ifv
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_ifv = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_ifv = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_ifv
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_ifv
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_ifv value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_ifv
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_ifv = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_ifv = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_ifv = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_ifv = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_ifv = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_ifv = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_ifv = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_ifv
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_ifv
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_ifv
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_ifv = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_ifv = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@medium_tank_flame_chassis value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_flame_chassis }
		send_equipment = { type = medium_tank_flame_chassis amount = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_flame_chassis }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_ifv = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_ifv = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 8 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ifv_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_ifv = yes
	}
}
