COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_towed_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_towed_artillery = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 9 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_towed_artillery = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_towed_artillery value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_towed_artillery
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_towed_artillery = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_towed_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_towed_artillery
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_towed_artillery value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_towed_artillery
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_towed_artillery
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_towed_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_towed_artillery = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_towed_artillery = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_towed_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_towed_artillery = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_towed_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_towed_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_towed_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_towed_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_towed_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_towed_artillery = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_towed_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_towed_artillery
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_towed_artillery value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_towed_artillery
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_towed_artillery = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_towed_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_towed_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_towed_artillery
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_towed_artillery value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_towed_artillery
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_towed_artillery = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_towed_artillery = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_towed_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_towed_artillery = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_towed_artillery = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_towed_artillery = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_towed_artillery = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_towed_artillery
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_towed_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_towed_artillery
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_towed_artillery = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_towed_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@artillery_equipment value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
		send_equipment = { type = artillery_equipment amount = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_towed_artillery = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_towed_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 9 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_towed_artillery_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_towed_artillery = yes
	}
}

			limit = { exists = yes }

		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_towed_artillery = yes
		}
	}
	AZE = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_towed_artillery = yes
		}
	}
	GEO = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_towed_artillery = yes
		}
	}
	KAZ = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_towed_artillery = yes

	KYR = {

			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_towed_artillery = yes
		}
	}
	TAJ = {

			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_towed_artillery = yes
		}
	}
	TRK = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_towed_artillery = yes
		}
	}
	UZB = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_towed_artillery = yes
		}

	MLV = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_towed_artillery = yes
		}
	}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_sp_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_sp_artillery = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 10 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_sp_artillery = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_sp_artillery value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_sp_artillery
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_sp_artillery = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_sp_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_sp_artillery
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_sp_artillery value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_sp_artillery
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_sp_artillery
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_sp_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_sp_artillery = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_sp_artillery = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_sp_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_sp_artillery = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_sp_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_sp_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_sp_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_sp_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_sp_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_sp_artillery = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_sp_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_sp_artillery
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_sp_artillery value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_sp_artillery
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_sp_artillery = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_sp_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_sp_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_sp_artillery
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_sp_artillery value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_sp_artillery
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_sp_artillery = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_sp_artillery = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_sp_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_sp_artillery = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_sp_artillery = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_sp_artillery = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_sp_artillery = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_sp_artillery
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_sp_artillery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_sp_artillery
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_sp_artillery = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_sp_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@medium_tank_artillery_chassis value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_artillery_chassis }
		send_equipment = { type = medium_tank_artillery_chassis amount = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_artillery_chassis }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_sp_artillery = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_sp_artillery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 10 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sp_artillery_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_sp_artillery = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_mlrs = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_mlrs = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 11 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_mlrs = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_mlrs value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_mlrs
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_mlrs = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_mlrs
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_mlrs
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_mlrs value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_mlrs
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_mlrs
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_mlrs = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_mlrs = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_mlrs = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_mlrs = {

			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_mlrs = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_mlrs
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_mlrs
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_mlrs = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_mlrs
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_mlrs = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_mlrs = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_mlrs
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_mlrs
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_mlrs value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_mlrs
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_mlrs = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_mlrs = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_mlrs
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_mlrs
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_mlrs value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_mlrs
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_mlrs = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_mlrs = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_mlrs = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_mlrs = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_mlrs = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_mlrs = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_mlrs = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_mlrs
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_mlrs
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_mlrs
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_mlrs = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_mlrs = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@medium_tank_rocket_chassis value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_rocket_chassis }
		send_equipment = { type = medium_tank_rocket_chassis amount = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_rocket_chassis }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_mlrs = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_mlrs = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 11 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_mlrs_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_mlrs = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_aa = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_aa = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 12 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_aa = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_aa value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_aa
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_aa = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_aa_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_aa_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_aa
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_aa
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_aa value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_aa
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_aa
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_aa = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_aa = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_aa_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_aa = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_aa = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_aa = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_aa_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_aa
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_aa
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_aa = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_aa
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_aa = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_aa = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_aa
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_aa
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_aa value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_aa
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_aa = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_aa = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_aa
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_aa
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_aa value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_aa
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_aa = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_aa = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_aa = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_aa = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_aa = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_aa = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_aa = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_aa
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_aa
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_aa
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_aa = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}

	if = {
		limit = {

			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@AA_Equipment value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_aa_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_delivered }

		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@AA_Equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_aa_consignment }

		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_spaa
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_spaa
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_spaa value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_spaa
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_spaa = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_spaa = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_spaa
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_spaa
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_spaa value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_spaa
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_spaa = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_spaa = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_spaa = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_spaa = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_spaa = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_spaa = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_spaa = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_spaa
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_spaa
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_spaa
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_spaa = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_spaa = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@medium_tank_aa_chassis value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_aa_chassis }
		send_equipment = { type = medium_tank_aa_chassis amount = COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_tank_aa_chassis }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_spaa = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_spaa = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 13 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_spaa_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_spaa = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_light_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_light_at = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 14 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_at = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_at value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_at
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_at = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_at
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_at value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_at
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_at
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_light_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_at = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_at = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_light_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_at = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_light_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_at = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_light_at = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_at
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_at value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_at
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_light_at = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_light_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_at
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_at value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_at
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_light_at = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_light_at = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_light_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_light_at = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_light_at = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_at = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_light_at = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_at
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_at
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_light_at = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_state = 2 }

		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@L_AT_Equipment }

		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@L_AT_Equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_at_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_at = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_heavy_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_heavy_at = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 15 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_heavy_at = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_heavy_at value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_heavy_at
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_heavy_at = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_heavy_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_heavy_at
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_heavy_at value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_heavy_at
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_heavy_at
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_heavy_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_heavy_at = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_heavy_at = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_heavy_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_heavy_at = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_heavy_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_heavy_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_heavy_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_heavy_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_heavy_at = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_heavy_at = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_heavy_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_heavy_at
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_heavy_at value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_heavy_at
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_heavy_at = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_heavy_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_heavy_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_heavy_at
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_heavy_at value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_heavy_at
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_heavy_at = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_heavy_at = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_heavy_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_heavy_at = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_heavy_at = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_heavy_at = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_heavy_at = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_heavy_at
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_heavy_at
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_heavy_at
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_heavy_at = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_heavy_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@H_AT_Equipment value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@H_AT_Equipment }
		send_equipment = { type = H_AT_Equipment amount = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@H_AT_Equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_heavy_at = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_heavy_at = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 15 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_heavy_at_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_heavy_at = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_light_fighter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_light_fighter = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 16 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_fighter = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_fighter value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_fighter
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_fighter = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_fighter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_fighter
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_fighter value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_fighter
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_fighter
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_light_fighter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_fighter = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_fighter = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_light_fighter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_fighter = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_fighter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_fighter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_return_pending
	}
}

		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 5 }

		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_fighter = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_light_fighter = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_fighter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_fighter
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_fighter value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_fighter
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_light_fighter = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_light_fighter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_fighter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_fighter
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_fighter value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_fighter
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_light_fighter = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_light_fighter = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_light_fighter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_light_fighter = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_light_fighter = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_fighter = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_light_fighter = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_fighter
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_fighter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_fighter
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_light_fighter = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_light_fighter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@small_plane_airframe value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@small_plane_airframe }
		send_equipment = { type = small_plane_airframe amount = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@small_plane_airframe }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_fighter = yes
	}
}

	}
	KYR = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_light_fighter = yes
		}
	}
	TAJ = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_light_fighter = yes
		}
	}
	TRK = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_light_fighter = yes
		}
	}
	UZB = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_light_fighter = yes
		}
	}
	MLV = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_light_fighter = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_light_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_light_multirole = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 17 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_multirole = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_multirole value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_multirole
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_multirole = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_multirole
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_multirole value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_multirole
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_multirole
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_light_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_multirole = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_multirole = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_light_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_multirole = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_light_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_light_multirole = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_multirole
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_multirole value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_multirole
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_light_multirole = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_light_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_multirole
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_multirole value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_multirole
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_light_multirole = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_light_multirole = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_light_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_light_multirole = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_light_multirole = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_multirole = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_light_multirole = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_multirole
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_multirole
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_light_multirole = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_light_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@small_plane_strike_airframe value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@small_plane_strike_airframe }
		send_equipment = { type = small_plane_strike_airframe amount = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@small_plane_strike_airframe }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_multirole = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_light_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 17 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_multirole_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_multirole = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_light_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_light_cas = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 18 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_cas = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_cas value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_cas
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_cas = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_cas
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_cas value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_cas
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_cas
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_light_cas = {

		limit = {
