			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_light_cas = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_light_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_cas = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_light_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_light_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_light_cas = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_cas
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_cas value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_light_cas
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_light_cas = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_light_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_cas
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_cas value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_light_cas
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_light_cas = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_light_cas = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_light_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_light_cas = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_light_cas = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_cas = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_light_cas = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_light_cas
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_light_cas
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_light_cas = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_light_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@small_plane_cas_airframe value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@small_plane_cas_airframe }
		send_equipment = { type = small_plane_cas_airframe amount = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@small_plane_cas_airframe }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_cas = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_light_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 18 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_light_cas_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_light_cas = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_multirole = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 19 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_multirole = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_multirole value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_multirole
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_multirole = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_multirole
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_multirole value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_multirole
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_multirole
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_multirole = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_multirole = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_multirole = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_multirole = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_multirole
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_multirole value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_multirole
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_multirole = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_multirole
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_multirole value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_multirole
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_multirole = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_multirole = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_multirole = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_multirole = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_multirole = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_multirole = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_multirole
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_multirole
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_multirole
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_multirole = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_multirole = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@medium_plane_airframe value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_plane_airframe }
		send_equipment = { type = medium_plane_airframe amount = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_plane_airframe }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_multirole_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_multirole = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_air_superiority = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_air_superiority = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 20 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_air_superiority = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_air_superiority value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_air_superiority
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_air_superiority = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_air_superiority
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_air_superiority
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_air_superiority value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_air_superiority
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_air_superiority
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_air_superiority = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_air_superiority = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_air_superiority = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_air_superiority = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_air_superiority = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_air_superiority
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_air_superiority
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_air_superiority = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_air_superiority
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_air_superiority = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_air_superiority = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_air_superiority
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_air_superiority
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_air_superiority value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_air_superiority
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_air_superiority = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_air_superiority = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_air_superiority
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_air_superiority
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_air_superiority value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_air_superiority
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_air_superiority = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_air_superiority = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_air_superiority = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_air_superiority = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_air_superiority = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_air_superiority = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_air_superiority = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_air_superiority
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_air_superiority
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_air_superiority
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_air_superiority = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_air_superiority = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@medium_plane_fighter_airframe value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_plane_fighter_airframe }
		send_equipment = { type = medium_plane_fighter_airframe amount = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_plane_fighter_airframe }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_air_superiority = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_air_superiority = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 20 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_superiority_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_air_superiority = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_cas = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 21 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_cas = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_cas value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_cas
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_cas = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cas_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cas_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_cas
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_cas value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_cas
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cas
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_cas = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cas_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_cas = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_cas = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cas_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_cas = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_cas
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_cas value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_cas
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_cas = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_cas
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_cas value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_cas
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_cas = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_cas = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_cas = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_cas = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_cas = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_cas = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cas
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_cas
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_cas
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_cas = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_cas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@medium_plane_cas_airframe value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_plane_cas_airframe }
		send_equipment = { type = medium_plane_cas_airframe amount = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@medium_plane_cas_airframe }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_cas_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_cas = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_air_transport = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_air_transport = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 22 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_air_transport = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_air_transport value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_air_transport
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_air_transport = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_air_transport
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_air_transport
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_air_transport value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_air_transport
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_air_transport
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_air_transport = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_air_transport = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_air_transport = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_air_transport = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_air_transport = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_air_transport
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_air_transport
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_air_transport = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_air_transport
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_air_transport = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_air_transport = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_air_transport
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_air_transport
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_air_transport value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_air_transport
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_air_transport = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_air_transport = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_air_transport
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_air_transport
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_air_transport value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_air_transport
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_air_transport = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_air_transport = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_air_transport = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_air_transport = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_air_transport = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_air_transport = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_air_transport = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_air_transport
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_air_transport
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_air_transport
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_air_transport = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_air_transport = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@large_plane_air_transport_airframe value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@large_plane_air_transport_airframe }
		send_equipment = { type = large_plane_air_transport_airframe amount = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@large_plane_air_transport_airframe }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_air_transport = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_air_transport = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 22 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_air_transport_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_air_transport = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_attack_helicopter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_attack_helicopter = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 23 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_attack_helicopter = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_attack_helicopter value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_attack_helicopter
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_attack_helicopter = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_attack_helicopter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_attack_helicopter
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_attack_helicopter value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_attack_helicopter
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_attack_helicopter
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_close_attack_helicopter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_state = 2 }

				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_attack_helicopter = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_attack_helicopter = yes

		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_certified

}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_attack_helicopter = {

		limit = {

			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@heavy_tank_chassis value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@heavy_tank_chassis }
		send_equipment = { type = heavy_tank_chassis amount = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@heavy_tank_chassis }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_helicopter_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_attack_helicopter = yes
	}
}

	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_transport_helicopter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_transport_helicopter = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 24 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_transport_helicopter = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_transport_helicopter value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_transport_helicopter
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_transport_helicopter = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_transport_helicopter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_transport_helicopter
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_transport_helicopter value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_transport_helicopter
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_transport_helicopter
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_transport_helicopter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_transport_helicopter = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_transport_helicopter = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_transport_helicopter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_transport_helicopter = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_transport_helicopter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_transport_helicopter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_transport_helicopter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_transport_helicopter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_transport_helicopter = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_transport_helicopter = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_transport_helicopter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_transport_helicopter
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_transport_helicopter value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_transport_helicopter
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_transport_helicopter = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_transport_helicopter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_transport_helicopter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_transport_helicopter
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_transport_helicopter value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_transport_helicopter
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_transport_helicopter = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_transport_helicopter = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_transport_helicopter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_transport_helicopter = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_transport_helicopter = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_transport_helicopter = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_transport_helicopter = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_transport_helicopter
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_transport_helicopter
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_transport_helicopter
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_transport_helicopter = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_transport_helicopter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@heavy_tank_amphibious_chassis value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@heavy_tank_amphibious_chassis }
		send_equipment = { type = heavy_tank_amphibious_chassis amount = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@heavy_tank_amphibious_chassis }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_transport_helicopter = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_transport_helicopter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 24 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_transport_helicopter_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_transport_helicopter = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_patrol = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_patrol = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 25 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_patrol = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_patrol value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_patrol
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_patrol = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_patrol
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_patrol
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_patrol value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_patrol
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_patrol
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_patrol = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_patrol = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_patrol = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_patrol = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_patrol = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_patrol
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_patrol
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_patrol = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_patrol
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_patrol = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_patrol = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_patrol
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_patrol
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_patrol value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_patrol
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_patrol = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_patrol = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_patrol
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_patrol
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_patrol value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_patrol
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_patrol = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_patrol = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_patrol = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_patrol = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_patrol = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_patrol = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_patrol = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_patrol
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_patrol
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_patrol
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_patrol = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_patrol = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 25 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_patrol_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_patrol = yes
	}
}

		if = {

			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_patrol = yes
		}

	UKR = {

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_corvette = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_corvette = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 26 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_corvette = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_corvette value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_corvette
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_corvette = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_corvette
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_corvette
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_corvette value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_corvette
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_corvette
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_corvette = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_corvette = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_corvette = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_corvette = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_corvette = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_corvette
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_corvette
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_corvette = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_corvette
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_corvette = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_corvette = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_corvette
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_corvette
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_corvette value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_corvette
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_corvette = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_corvette = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_corvette
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_corvette_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_corvette
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_corvette value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_corvette
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_corvette = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_corvette = yes }
}

			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_frigate value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_frigate
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_frigate = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_frigate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_frigate
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_frigate
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_frigate value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_frigate
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_frigate = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_frigate = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_frigate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_frigate = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_frigate = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_frigate = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_frigate = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_frigate
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_frigate
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_frigate
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_frigate = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_frigate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 27 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_frigate_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_frigate = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_frigate = {
	SOV = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_frigate = yes
		}
	}
	UKR = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_frigate = yes
		}
	}
	BLR = {

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_destroyer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_destroyer = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 28 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_destroyer = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_destroyer value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_destroyer
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_destroyer = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_destroyer
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_destroyer
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_destroyer value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_destroyer
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_destroyer
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_destroyer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_destroyer = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_destroyer = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_destroyer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_destroyer = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_destroyer
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_destroyer
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_destroyer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_destroyer
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_destroyer = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_destroyer = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_destroyer
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_destroyer_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_destroyer
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_destroyer value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_destroyer
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_destroyer = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
