		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_cruiser = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cruiser
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_cruiser
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_cruiser value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_cruiser
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_cruiser = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_cruiser = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cruiser
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_cruiser
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_cruiser value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_cruiser
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_cruiser = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_cruiser = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_cruiser = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_cruiser = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_cruiser = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_cruiser = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_cruiser = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cruiser
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_cruiser
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_cruiser
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_cruiser = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_cruiser = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 29 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cruiser_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_cruiser = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_carrier = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_carrier = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 30 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_carrier = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_carrier value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_carrier
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_carrier = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_carrier
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_carrier
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_carrier value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_carrier
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_carrier
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_carrier = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_carrier = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_carrier = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_carrier = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_carrier = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_carrier
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_carrier
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_carrier = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_carrier
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_carrier = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_carrier = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_carrier
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_carrier
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_carrier value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_carrier
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_carrier = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_attack_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_attack_submarine = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 31 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_attack_submarine = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_attack_submarine value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_attack_submarine
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_attack_submarine = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_attack_submarine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_attack_submarine
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_attack_submarine value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_attack_submarine
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_attack_submarine
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_attack_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_attack_submarine = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_attack_submarine = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_attack_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_attack_submarine = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_attack_submarine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_attack_submarine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_attack_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_attack_submarine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_attack_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_attack_submarine = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_attack_submarine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_attack_submarine
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_attack_submarine value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_attack_submarine
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_attack_submarine = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_attack_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_attack_submarine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_attack_submarine
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_attack_submarine value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_attack_submarine
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_attack_submarine = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_attack_submarine = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_attack_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_attack_submarine = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_attack_submarine = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_attack_submarine = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_attack_submarine = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_attack_submarine
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_attack_submarine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_attack_submarine
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_attack_submarine = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_noop = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_certify_attack_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_kind = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_family = 31 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_return_serial = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_serial }
			}
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_return_pending
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_certified
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_attack_submarine = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_missile_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_missile_submarine = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 32 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_missile_submarine = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_missile_submarine value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_missile_submarine
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_missile_submarine = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_missile_submarine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_missile_submarine
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_missile_submarine value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_missile_submarine
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_missile_submarine
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_missile_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_missile_submarine = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_missile_submarine = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_missile_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_missile_submarine = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_missile_submarine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_missile_submarine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_missile_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_missile_submarine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_return_pending
	}
}

		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_return_pending

		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_missile_submarine
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_missile_submarine value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_missile_submarine
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_missile_submarine = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}

			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes

COLDWAR_ORD_close_missile_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 2 }

			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_missile_submarine = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_missile_submarine = yes

			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_missile_submarine = yes }

		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_missile_submarine
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_missile_submarine

		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_certified
	}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_convoy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_convoy = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 33 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_convoy = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_convoy value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_convoy
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_convoy = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_convoy
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_convoy
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_convoy value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_convoy
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_baseline = total_equipment_produced_convoy }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_target = total_equipment_produced_convoy }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_target = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_quantity }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_convoy
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_convoy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_convoy = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_convoy = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_convoy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_convoy = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_convoy
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_convoy
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_convoy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_convoy
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_convoy = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_convoy = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_convoy
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_convoy
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_convoy value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_convoy
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_convoy = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_convoy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_convoy
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_convoy
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_convoy value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_convoy
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_convoy = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_convoy = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_convoy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_convoy = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_convoy = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_convoy = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_convoy = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_convoy
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_convoy
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_convoy
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_convoy = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_produced = total_equipment_produced_convoy }
	subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_produced = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_baseline }
	clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_produced min = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_convoy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@convoy value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@convoy }
		send_equipment = { type = convoy amount = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@convoy }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_convoy_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_convoy = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_convoy = {
	SOV = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_convoy = yes
		}
	}
	UKR = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }

	BLR = {

		}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_guided_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_guided_missile = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 34 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_guided_missile = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_guided_missile value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_guided_missile
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_guided_missile = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_guided_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_guided_missile
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_guided_missile value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_guided_missile
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_baseline = total_equipment_produced_missile }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_target = total_equipment_produced_missile }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_target = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_quantity }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_guided_missile
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_guided_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_guided_missile = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_guided_missile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_guided_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_guided_missile = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_guided_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_guided_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_guided_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_guided_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_guided_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_guided_missile = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_guided_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_guided_missile
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_guided_missile value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_guided_missile
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_guided_missile = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_guided_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_guided_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_guided_missile
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_guided_missile value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_guided_missile
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_guided_missile = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_guided_missile = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_guided_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_guided_missile = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_guided_missile = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_guided_missile = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_guided_missile = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_guided_missile
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_guided_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_guided_missile
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_guided_missile = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_produced = total_equipment_produced_missile }
	subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_produced = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_baseline }
	clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_produced min = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_guided_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@guided_missile_equipment value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@guided_missile_equipment }
		send_equipment = { type = guided_missile_equipment amount = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@guided_missile_equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_guided_missile_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_guided_missile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_ballistic_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_ballistic_missile = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 35 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_ballistic_missile = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_ballistic_missile value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_ballistic_missile
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_ballistic_missile = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_ballistic_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_ballistic_missile
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_ballistic_missile value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_ballistic_missile
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_baseline = total_equipment_produced_ballistic_missile }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_target = total_equipment_produced_ballistic_missile }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_target = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_quantity }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_ballistic_missile
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_ballistic_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_ballistic_missile = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_ballistic_missile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_ballistic_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_ballistic_missile = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_ballistic_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_ballistic_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_ballistic_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_ballistic_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_ballistic_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_ballistic_missile = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_ballistic_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_ballistic_missile
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_ballistic_missile value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_ballistic_missile
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_ballistic_missile = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_ballistic_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_ballistic_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_ballistic_missile
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_ballistic_missile value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_ballistic_missile
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_ballistic_missile = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_ballistic_missile = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_ballistic_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_ballistic_missile = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_ballistic_missile = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_ballistic_missile = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_ballistic_missile = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_ballistic_missile
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_ballistic_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_ballistic_missile
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_ballistic_missile = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_produced = total_equipment_produced_ballistic_missile }
	subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_produced = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_baseline }
	clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_produced min = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_ballistic_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@ballistic_missile_equipment value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@ballistic_missile_equipment }
		send_equipment = { type = ballistic_missile_equipment amount = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@ballistic_missile_equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_ballistic_missile_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_ballistic_missile = yes
	}
}

		if = {

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_sam_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_sam_missile = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 36 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 2 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_sam_missile = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_sam_missile value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_sam_missile
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_sam_missile = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_sam_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_sam_missile
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_sam_missile value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_sam_missile
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_baseline = total_equipment_produced_sam_missile }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_target = total_equipment_produced_sam_missile }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_target = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_quantity }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_sam_missile
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV

			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_sam_missile = yes
	}
}

	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_sam_missile = {
	if = {

		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_sam_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_sam_missile
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_sam_missile value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_sam_missile
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_sam_missile = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_sam_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_sam_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_sam_missile
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_sam_missile value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_sam_missile
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_sam_missile = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_sam_missile = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_sam_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_sam_missile = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_sam_missile = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_sam_missile = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_sam_missile = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_sam_missile
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_sam_missile
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_sam_missile
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_sam_missile = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_produced = total_equipment_produced_sam_missile }
	subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_produced = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_baseline }
	clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_produced min = 0 }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_sam_missile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_kind = 2 }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@sam_missile_equipment value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_consignment = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_delivered }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@sam_missile_equipment }
		send_equipment = { type = sam_missile_equipment amount = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_consignment target = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = SOV.num_equipment@sam_missile_equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery = COLDWAR_ORD_before_delivery }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery min = 0 max = COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_consignment }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_sam_missile_delivered = COLDWAR_ORD_actual_delivery }
		COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_sam_missile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_artillery_class = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_artillery_class = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 37 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 1 }
				}
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_artillery_class = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_artillery_class value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_artillery_class
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_artillery_class = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_artillery_class
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_artillery_class
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_artillery_class value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_artillery_class
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_baseline = total_equipment_produced_artillery }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_target = total_equipment_produced_artillery }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_target = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_quantity }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_artillery_class
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_artillery_class = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_artillery_class = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_artillery_class = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_artillery_class = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_artillery_class = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_artillery_class
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_artillery_class
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_artillery_class = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_artillery_class
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_artillery_class = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_artillery_class = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_artillery_class
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_artillery_class
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_artillery_class value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_artillery_class
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_artillery_class = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_artillery_class = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_artillery_class
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_artillery_class
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_artillery_class value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_artillery_class
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_artillery_class = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_artillery_class = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_artillery_class = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_artillery_class = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_artillery_class = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_artillery_class = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_artillery_class = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_artillery_class
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_artillery_class
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_artillery_class
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_artillery_class = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_produced = total_equipment_produced_artillery }
	subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_produced = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_baseline }
	clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_artillery_class_produced min = 0 }
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_civilian = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 38 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 3 }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_civilian = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_civilian value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_civilian
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_civilian = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_civilian
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_civilian
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_civilian value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_civilian
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_baseline0 = 0 }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV is_core_of = PREV }
			add_to_variable = { PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_baseline0 = building_level@industrial_complex }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_target0 = COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_baseline0 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_target0 = COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_quantity }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_civilian
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}

		limit = {

		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_civilian
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_civilian
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_civilian value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_civilian
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_civilian = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_civilian = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_civilian = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_civilian = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_civilian = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_civilian = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_civilian
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_civilian
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_civilian
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_civilian = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_current0 = 0 }
	every_owned_state = {
		limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV is_core_of = PREV }
		add_to_variable = { PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_civilian_current0 = building_level@industrial_complex }
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_civilian = {

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_military = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_military = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 39 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 3 }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_military_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_military_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_military_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_military = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_military value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_military
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_military = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_military_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_military_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_military_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_military_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_military_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_military
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_military
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_military value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_military
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_baseline0 = 0 }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV is_core_of = PREV }
			add_to_variable = { PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_military_baseline0 = building_level@arms_factory }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_target0 = COLDWAR_ORD_military_baseline0 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_target0 = COLDWAR_ORD_military_quantity }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_military
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_military = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_military = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_military_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_military = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_military = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_military = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_military_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_military
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_military
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_military_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_military = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_military
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_military_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_military = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_military = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_military
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_military_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_military
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_military value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_military
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_military = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_military = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_military
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_military_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_military
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_military value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_military
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_military = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_military = yes }
}

			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_military_state > 0 }
