COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_agriculture = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_agriculture = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 40 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 3 }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_agriculture = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_agriculture value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_agriculture
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_agriculture = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_agriculture
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_agriculture
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_agriculture value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_agriculture
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_baseline0 = 0 }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV is_core_of = PREV }
			add_to_variable = { PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_baseline0 = building_level@agriculture_district }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_target0 = COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_baseline0 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_target0 = COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_quantity }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_agriculture
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}

			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_agriculture
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_agriculture = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_agriculture = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_agriculture = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_agriculture = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_agriculture = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_agriculture = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_agriculture = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_agriculture
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_agriculture
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_agriculture
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_agriculture = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_current0 = 0 }
	every_owned_state = {
		limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV is_core_of = PREV }
		add_to_variable = { PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_agriculture_current0 = building_level@agriculture_district }
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_agriculture = {
	SOV = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_agriculture = yes

	UKR = {

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_cotton = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_cotton = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 41 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 3 }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_cotton = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_cotton value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_cotton
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_cotton = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_cotton
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_cotton
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_cotton value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_cotton
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_baseline0 = 0 }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV is_core_of = PREV }
			add_to_variable = { PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_baseline0 = building_level@agriculture_district }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_baseline1 = 0 }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV is_core_of = PREV }
			add_to_variable = { PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_baseline1 = building_level@industrial_complex }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_target0 = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_baseline0 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_target0 = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_target1 = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_baseline1 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_target1 = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_quantity }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cotton
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_cotton = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_cotton = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_cotton = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_cotton = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_cotton = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_cotton
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_cotton
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_cotton = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cotton
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_cotton = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_cotton = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cotton
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_cotton
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_cotton value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_cotton
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_cotton = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_fail_cotton = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cotton
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_cotton
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_cotton value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_cotton
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_cotton = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_cotton = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_cotton = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_cotton = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_cotton = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_cotton = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_cotton = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_cotton
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_cotton
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_cotton
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_cotton_certified
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_fail_fossil = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_fossil_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_fossil_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_fossil
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_fossil_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_fossil
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_fossil value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_fossil
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_fossil = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_fossil = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_fossil = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_fossil_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_fossil_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_fossil = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_fossil = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_fossil = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_fossil = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_fossil
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_fossil
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_fossil
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_fossil_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_fossil_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_fossil_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_fossil = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_fossil_current0 = 0 }
	every_owned_state = {
		limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV is_core_of = PREV }
		add_to_variable = { PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_fossil_current0 = building_level@fossil_powerplant }
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_fossil = {
	SOV = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_fossil = yes
		}
	}
	UKR = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_fossil = yes
		}
	}
	BLR = {

			limit = { exists = yes }

		}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_infrastructure = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_infrastructure = yes
			always = yes
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 43 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 3 }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_infrastructure = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_infrastructure value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_infrastructure
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_infrastructure = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			always = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_infrastructure
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_infrastructure
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_infrastructure value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_infrastructure
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_baseline0 = 0 }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV is_core_of = PREV }
			add_to_variable = { PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_baseline0 = building_level@infrastructure }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_target0 = COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_baseline0 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_target0 = COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_quantity }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_infrastructure
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_approve_infrastructure = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = SOV
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_infrastructure = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_serial }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 1 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_ORD_activate_infrastructure = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_reject_infrastructure = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 1 }
			ROOT = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = SOV
					COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_infrastructure = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_serial }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOT = { tag = SOV }
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 0 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_infrastructure
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_infrastructure
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_infrastructure = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 5 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_infrastructure
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_return_pending
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_infrastructure = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_infrastructure = yes }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 3 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_infrastructure
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_infrastructure
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_infrastructure value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_infrastructure
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_infrastructure = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_reserve_dockyard = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_free_dockyard = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			PREV = {
				COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_family = 44 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind = 3 }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = PREV.id }
				OR = {
					tag = SOV
					check_variable = { THIS.id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_quantity = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_quantity }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_fraction = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_fraction }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_kind = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_kind }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_origin = PREV.id }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_delivered = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial = 1 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_serial = global.COLDWAR_ORD_next_serial }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_fraction }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_certified
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_return_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_origin = SOV.id }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_activate_dockyard = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 1 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_dockyard value = 1 days = 30 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_dockyard
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_activate_dockyard = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_cost = SOV.gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_cost = COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_fraction }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV }
			check_variable = { naval_factory_total > 0 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 1 }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_fraction = 0 }
				SOV = {
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_cost value = .00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = { value = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_cost multiply = -1 }
				}
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_cost }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_dockyard
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_dockyard
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 2 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_dockyard value = 1 days = 365 }
		activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_dockyard
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_baseline0 = 0 }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV is_core_of = PREV }
			add_to_variable = { PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_baseline0 = building_level@dockyard }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_target0 = COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_baseline0 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_target0 = COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_quantity }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_dockyard
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 6 }
		}
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 2 }
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 4 }
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_dockyard
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_return_pending
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed
		SOV = {
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_dockyard
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_dockyard value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_dockyard
			COLDWAR_ORD_outcome_changed_dockyard = yes
			remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock value = 1 days = 180 }
			activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock
			COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes
			COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		}
	}
	else = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_dockyard = yes }
}
COLDWAR_ORD_close_dockyard = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state > 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 2 }
			}
			COLDWAR_ORD_measure_dockyard = yes
			if = {
				limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_ready_dockyard = yes }
				COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_dockyard = yes
			}
			else = { COLDWAR_ORD_fail_dockyard = yes }
		}
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_objective_dockyard
		remove_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_window_dockyard
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_window_dockyard
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_return_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_certified
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_measure_dockyard = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_current0 = 0 }
	every_owned_state = {
		limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV is_core_of = PREV }
		add_to_variable = { PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_current0 = building_level@dockyard }
	}
}

	SOV = {

			limit = { exists = yes }

COLDWAR_ORD_sync_rifles = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_rifles }
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_rifles
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_rifles }
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_rifles
	}
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_failure_remaining = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_success_remaining = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			SOV = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_rifles }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_candidate_remaining = SOV.days_mission_timeout@COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_rifles }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_candidate_remaining value = COLDWAR_ORD_success_remaining compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_success_remaining = COLDWAR_ORD_candidate_remaining }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			SOV = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_rifles }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_candidate_remaining = SOV.days_mission_timeout@COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_rifles }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_candidate_remaining value = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_remaining compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_failure_remaining = COLDWAR_ORD_candidate_remaining }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_member = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_failure_remaining > 0 }
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_rifles days = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_remaining }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_success_remaining > 0 }
				}
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_rifles days = COLDWAR_ORD_success_remaining }
			}
		}
	}
}

		limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_command }

		}

				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_failure_remaining > 0 }
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_agriculture days = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_remaining }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_success_remaining > 0 }
				}
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_agriculture days = COLDWAR_ORD_success_remaining }
			}
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_sync_cotton = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_cotton }
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_cotton
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_cotton }
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_cotton
	}
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_failure_remaining = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_success_remaining = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			SOV = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_cotton }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_candidate_remaining = SOV.days_mission_timeout@COLDWAR_ORD_success_clock_cotton }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_candidate_remaining value = COLDWAR_ORD_success_remaining compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_success_remaining = COLDWAR_ORD_candidate_remaining }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			SOV = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_cotton }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_candidate_remaining = SOV.days_mission_timeout@COLDWAR_ORD_failure_clock_cotton }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_ORD_candidate_remaining value = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_remaining compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_failure_remaining = COLDWAR_ORD_candidate_remaining }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_ORD_member = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_failure_remaining > 0 }
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_cotton days = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_remaining }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_success_remaining > 0 }
				}
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_cotton days = COLDWAR_ORD_success_remaining }
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = {
	set_variable = { global.COLDWAR_UNI_members = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.COLDWAR_UNI_fulfilled = 0 }
	UKR = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_scored }
			add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_UNI_members = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_success
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_calendar_failed }
				}
				add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_UNI_fulfilled = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	BLR = {

			}
		}
	}

		}
	}
	set_variable = { global.COLDWAR_UNI_needed = global.COLDWAR_UNI_members }
	multiply_variable = { global.COLDWAR_UNI_needed = 9 }
	divide_variable = { global.COLDWAR_UNI_needed = 14 }
	add_to_variable = { global.COLDWAR_UNI_needed = 0.49999 }
	round_variable = global.COLDWAR_UNI_needed
	clr_global_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_year_won
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.COLDWAR_UNI_members > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = global.COLDWAR_UNI_fulfilled value = global.COLDWAR_UNI_needed compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_global_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_year_won
	}
}
COLDWAR_ORD_settle_calendar = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_enabled }
		set_variable = { global.COLDWAR_UNI_year_plan = global.COLDWAR_SOV_plan }
		COLDWAR_ORD_tally_calendar = yes
		set_variable = { global.COLDWAR_UNI_last_members = global.COLDWAR_UNI_members }
		set_variable = { global.COLDWAR_UNI_last_fulfilled = global.COLDWAR_UNI_fulfilled }
